# 北境雄心：圖片生成清單

## 怎麼用

1. 每一張都先貼上「共用風格」，再接那一張自己的描述。這樣所有角色的畫風會一致，也會和現在的主角立繪對得上。
2. 每次在對話裡傳 5 張給我，照清單編號的順序傳，並在訊息裡寫上編號，例如「1-5」。
3. 尺寸：
   - 人物：直式 3:4，系統裡用 1024×1536 或最接近的直式就好。
   - 場景：橫式 16:9。
   - 我會自己縮圖、轉檔，你不用處理。
4. 某張畫壞了（多手指、文字、現代物品），重畫那一張就好。還沒到的圖，遊戲裡會先用符文圖示代替，不影響遊玩。

## ★ v0.14 劇情補圖（226 張）

程式都做好了：每張圖一到，放進遊戲就會自動出現在對的地方。圖還沒到之前，會先用現有的圖或立繪代替。

建議順序：A → B → C → J → K → M → D → E → F → G → H → I。I 組最多，可以最後慢慢補。

**主角規則**：除了「夢見母親」，主角都是背影或陰影，戴兜帽、看不出男女。只有稱王之後的場景才戴王冠。

```
A busy Viking trading dock. Hrolf (late twenties, the arrogant son of a jarl, red-brown hair tied back, a fur collar and red cloak over mail, a sneering mouth) has just kicked over a wooden crate at the feet of the young player character (seen from behind, hooded, a plain wool tunic, no crown), spilling dried fish; he laughs loudly with his thugs from a red-sailed ship behind him. Dockworkers stop and stare. Humiliation, tension.
```

**1. sc_oath_hakon（與哈康立誓）**

```

Cinematic photorealistic digital painting, Viking Age Scandinavia (9th–10th century). Dramatic golden-hour light with cold blue shadows, atmospheric mist, epic sense of scale. Wide 16:9 landscape composition with room at the top for a title. No text, no letters, no watermark, no frame, no modern objects.
If the player character appears, show them only from behind or in shadow (face hidden, gender not identifiable: a deep fur-lined hood, heavy fur cloak, no crown unless the scene says they are king).
Character: An old one-eyed Viking veteran in his sixties, leather eyepatch, long grey beard in two braids, a missing front tooth showing in a crooked grin, weathered skin full of scars, a battered round shield covered in sword cuts on his back, worn chainmail under a wolf-fur cloak. Tired but proud eyes.
Scene of an oath of loyalty inside the player's longhall by firelight, based on this moment from the game (Chinese): 「哈康把那面滿是刀痕的盾牌靠在牆上，坐到你對面。「我跟過三個首領。兩個死在我前面，一個是我親手埋的。」他割破手掌，把血抹在你的斧刃上。「你是最後一個。」」 Show 哈康's gesture from this moment clearly. Solemn, personal, a bond for life.
```

**14. sc_oath_sigrid0（與西格麗德立誓：還沒稱王的版本）**

西格麗德可能在你稱王之前就立誓，那時候不能有王冠。最簡單的做法：把 sc_oath_sigrid 那張丟回給 GPT，請它照下面改。

```
Edit the attached image (sc_oath_sigrid): keep Sigrid, her pose, the deer shield and the lighting exactly the same. Change only these: remove the crown from the hooded player character (a plain deep fur hood, no crown, no circlet); make the hall a humbler wooden longhouse instead of a royal hall (fewer banners, no throne-like carvings, rougher timber). The player stays seen from behind, face hidden, gender not identifiable. No text, no watermark.
```

```
Character: An old one-eyed Viking veteran in his sixties, leather eyepatch, long grey beard in two braids, a missing front tooth showing in a crooked grin, weathered skin full of scars, a battered round shield covered in sword cuts on his back, worn chainmail under a wolf-fur cloak. Tired but proud eyes.
The first time the player meets this character, before they join. The scene from the game (Chinese): 「一個獨眼的老戰士攔住你：「我看過你打架。我叫哈康，我的斧頭還沒老，只是缺個值得追隨的人。」」
```

**1. sc_rm_clash（兩軍撞在一起）**

```

Cinematic photorealistic digital painting, Viking Age Scandinavia (9th–10th century). Dramatic golden-hour light with cold blue shadows, atmospheric mist, epic sense of scale. Wide 16:9 landscape composition with room at the top for a title. No text, no letters, no watermark, no frame, no modern objects.
If the player character appears, show them only from behind or in shadow (face hidden, gender not identifiable: a deep fur-lined hood, heavy fur cloak; this is after they became king, so a simple iron crown over the hood is allowed).
Setting: Ragnarök, the end of the world. A vast frozen plain under a burning, cracked sky: frost giants from the north, fire giants from the south, ice pillars and flames everywhere, the mortal Viking army mixed with glowing golden einherjar (dead warriors returned from Valhalla).
The moment two armies collide: the mortal shield wall and the einherjar slam into a tide of frost and fire giants. Chaos, axes, shattered ice, sparks and blood in the snow, no single hero in focus. Overwhelming scale and noise.
```

**2. sc_rm_village（漁村少年拿魚叉擋在老兵前面）**

```

Cinematic photorealistic digital painting, Viking Age Scandinavia (9th–10th century). Dramatic golden-hour light with cold blue shadows, atmospheric mist, epic sense of scale. Wide 16:9 landscape composition with room at the top for a title. No text, no letters, no watermark, no frame, no modern objects.
If the player character appears, show them only from behind or in shadow (face hidden, gender not identifiable: a deep fur-lined hood, heavy fur cloak; this is after they became king, so a simple iron crown over the hood is allowed).
Setting: Ragnarök, the end of the world. A vast frozen plain under a burning, cracked sky: frost giants from the north, fire giants from the south, ice pillars and flames everywhere, the mortal Viking army mixed with glowing golden einherjar (dead warriors returned from Valhalla).
A skinny fishing-village boy of about sixteen, no armor, only a patched wool tunic and a fishing harpoon, stands his ground in front of a fallen, wounded old Viking veteran, facing a towering frost giant. Terrified but not stepping back. A small figure of courage in the middle of the end of the world.
```

**3. sc_rm_thorstein（托爾斯坦掩護新兵後退）**

```

Cinematic photorealistic digital painting, Viking Age Scandinavia (9th–10th century). Dramatic golden-hour light with cold blue shadows, atmospheric mist, epic sense of scale. Setting: Ragnarök, the end of the world. A vast frozen plain under a burning, cracked sky: frost giants from the north, fire giants from the south, ice pillars and flames everywhere, the mortal Viking army mixed with glowing golden einherjar (dead warriors returned from Valhalla).
Old Thorstein (a veteran Viking oarsman in his late fifties: a broad, heavy-shouldered man with a long grey beard in two thick braids, weathered sun-and-salt skin, deep crow's feet, a broken nose, huge calloused hands resting on the haft of an old notched bearded axe. Plain undyed wool tunic, a worn sealskin vest, a faded red-and-black arm ring from a royal ship he once rowed on.) holds an old round shield and his notched bearded axe, covering three frightened young recruits as they retreat behind him, while he stays last in line facing the giants, shouting at them to go. Grim, fatherly, unafraid.
```

### K. 諸神黃昏之後：尾聲（13 張，16:9）

打贏諸神黃昏之後、結局畫面之前的分鏡。神那 6 張依最眷顧你的神只出一張；勇士、家人、老王、托爾斯坦，有對應的人才會出現。

| # | 檔名 | 內容 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | sc_re_dawn | 天亮了，太陽還在 |
| 2 | sc_re_god_odin | 奧丁：預言裡沒有你 |
| 3 | sc_re_god_thor | 索爾：走了九步，又走了一步 |
| 4 | sc_re_god_tyr | 提爾：帳結清了 |
| 5 | sc_re_god_freyja | 弗蕾亞：眼淚變成了金子 |
| 6 | sc_re_god_loki | 洛基不見了，只剩一隻手套 |
| 7 | sc_re_god_none | 眾神從灰燼裡站起來 |
| 8 | sc_re_land | 新的綠色大地從海裡升起 |
| 9 | sc_re_champs | 活下來的勇士們 |
| 10 | sc_re_family | 家人在人群裡找到你 |
| 11 | sc_re_king | 老王回到彩虹橋上，回頭看你 |
| 12 | sc_re_thorstein | 托爾斯坦遞來一杯酒 |
| 13 | sc_re_skald | 幾十年後，詩人唱完了你的故事 |

**1. sc_re_dawn（天亮了，太陽還在）**

```

Cinematic photorealistic digital painting, Viking Age Scandinavia (9th–10th century). Dramatic golden-hour light with cold blue shadows, atmospheric mist, epic sense of scale. Wide 16:9 landscape composition with room at the top for a title. No text, no letters, no watermark, no frame, no modern objects.
If the player character appears, show them only from behind or in shadow (face hidden, gender not identifiable: a deep fur-lined hood, heavy fur cloak; this is after they became king, so a simple iron crown over the hood is allowed).
Setting: the morning after Ragnarök, the end of the world that did not end. The battle is over; the frozen plain is quiet, covered in ash, broken ice and fallen giants, but the light is soft and hopeful.
The first sunrise after the last battle: a golden sun rising slowly over the ash-covered frozen plain, its light falling on exhausted survivors, broken shields and the bodies of giants. The player (from behind, hooded, leaning on an axe) watches the sun. Silence, disbelief, relief.
```

```
If the player character appears, show them only from behind or in shadow (face hidden, gender not identifiable: a deep fur-lined hood, heavy fur cloak, a simple iron crown over the hood, and a small wooden cross pendant; they are a baptized Christian king).
```
```
Setting: Norway around the year 1000. The king has been baptized; the chieftains who still worship the old Norse gods have risen against them, led by Thorir 'the Hound', who wears a magical reindeer-skin coat enchanted by Sámi sorcerers (faint glowing blue runes on the fur).
```
```
The battle of Stiklestad (cross banners against raven banners, muddy snow): a proud young shieldmaiden with a dark blonde braid and a shield painted with a golden stag holds the left flank, shouting back to the king.
```

```
Cinematic photorealistic digital painting, Viking Age Scandinavia (9th–10th century). Dramatic golden-hour light with cold blue shadows, atmospheric mist, epic sense of scale. Wide 16:9 landscape composition with room at the top for a title. No text, no letters, no watermark, no frame, no modern objects.
Inside a Viking camp, a training duel has just ended. The shieldmaiden Astrid (late twenties, long thick blonde braid over one shoulder, sides of the head shaved with small braids, chainmail and leather vest, a thin scar across one eyebrow) stands with her left hand empty, staring at her round shield painted with a black raven, which lies in the mud a few steps away. She has turned her face slightly away from the viewer, jaw tight, the tips of her ears faintly red: surprised and embarrassed, trying not to show it. Onlookers stand frozen in silence. Restrained, human, a rare crack in a hard warrior.
```

**1. sc_rag_horn（海姆達爾吹響號角）**

```
Cinematic photorealistic digital painting, Viking Age Scandinavia (9th–10th century). Dramatic golden-hour light with cold blue shadows, atmospheric mist, epic sense of scale. Wide 16:9 landscape composition with room at the top for a title. No text, no letters, no watermark, no frame, no modern objects.
EPIC WIDE SHOT. At the top of the shimmering rainbow bridge Bifröst, at the very edge of Asgard, the watchman god Heimdall (tall, white-gold armor, golden teeth, long cloak) blows the enormous curved horn Gjallarhorn; the sound visibly ripples through the red clouds like a shockwave. Far below lies the frozen world, tiny and dark. Ominous, final, awe-inspiring.
```

**2. sc_rag_giants（巨人大軍全景）**

```
Cinematic photorealistic digital painting, Viking Age Scandinavia (9th–10th century). Dramatic golden-hour light with cold blue shadows, atmospheric mist, epic sense of scale. Wide 16:9 landscape composition with room at the top for a title. No text, no letters, no watermark, no frame, no modern objects.
EPIC WIDE SHOT. The Ragnarök battlefield: an endless frozen plain under a burning red sky, snow and embers in the air. The giant host fills the plain to the horizon, ALL facing toward the viewer: towering blue-grey frost giants marching from the north on the left, burning fire giants under a sky of flame on the right; among them, the silhouette of a gigantic wolf and, in the sea behind, the coils of a world serpent; a huge dark ship looms in the mist. The scale is overwhelming. Doom, dread.
```

**3. sc_rag_loki（洛基走在巨人最前面）**

```
Cinematic photorealistic digital painting, Viking Age Scandinavia (9th–10th century). Dramatic golden-hour light with cold blue shadows, atmospheric mist, epic sense of scale. Wide 16:9 landscape composition with room at the top for a title. No text, no letters, no watermark, no frame, no modern objects.
The Ragnarök battlefield: an endless frozen plain under a burning red sky, snow and embers in the air. At the very front of the giant army walks Loki: slender, sharp-featured, long red hair, thin scars across his lips, dark green and black clothes, no armor. He has turned to look back over his shoulder straight at the viewer with a knowing, almost friendly half-smile, as if greeting an old friend. Behind him, the endless ranks of frost and fire giants wait for his signal. Charming, unsettling, tragic.
```

```
Cinematic photorealistic digital painting, Viking Age Scandinavia (9th–10th century). Dramatic warm golden-hour rim light with cold blue shadows. Old King Halfdan of Norway: around seventy, long white beard and long white hair, the same gold knotwork crown, dark red-and-black wool robes embroidered with gold Norse knotwork, a heavy dark fur mantle over both shoulders, and his ancient royal longsword: long and heavy, notched and etched with faded runes, with a gold knotwork crossguard and a crowned wolf-head pommel. In a dark royal hall with dying torches. FIRST STAGE of his awakening: he has just risen to his feet, holding the sword upright in front of his chest with both hands; the first faint embers drift around him and his pale eyes are just beginning to glow with a cold light. His MOUTH IS CLOSED, lips pressed together, jaw set; his face is calm, cold and unreadable, not angry, not shouting, not snarling. The fur mantle is still on his shoulders. Controlled, dangerous, the storm only starting.
```

**R4. sc_york_3b（約克：兩頂王冠，誰也沒戴上）**（新，可選）

用在：打下約克但**沒有**扶植傀儡的結局。原本的 sc_york_3 是「有人跪著獻上王冠」，只適合扶植艾拉的路線。

```
Cinematic photorealistic digital painting, Viking Age Scandinavia (9th–10th century). Dramatic golden-hour light with cold blue shadows, atmospheric mist, epic sense of scale. Wide 16:9 landscape composition with room at the top for a title. No text, no letters, no watermark, no frame, no modern objects.
Inside captured York, 9th century: a ruined Roman hall with broken columns, a cracked mosaic floor and grey light falling through a collapsed roof. On a rough wooden table in the middle stand TWO different Anglo-Saxon royal crowns side by side, one slightly dented, both unclaimed; nobody kneels, nobody wears them. A Viking ruler seen from behind (face hidden, could be a man or a woman, fur cloak and a simple crown) stands a few steps away looking down at them; Viking warriors linger at the doorway; through the broken arches, the conquered town is quiet, smoke rising. Empty, ironic, a new order.
```

```
Cinematic photorealistic digital painting, Viking Age Scandinavia (9th–10th century). Dramatic golden-hour light with cold blue shadows, atmospheric mist, epic sense of scale. Wide 16:9 landscape composition with room at the top for a title. No text, no letters, no watermark, no frame, no modern objects.
An abandoned Anglo-Saxon royal hall in Wessex, 9th century, at dusk: a long timber hall with carved posts and hanging tapestries, the hearth fire in the middle still burning low, a long table with half-finished cups of ale, bread and an overturned bench, a fine cloak left behind on a chair; the great doors stand wide open. Viking warriors have just burst in with axes and torches and stop, confused, looking around the empty room. The king is gone. Eerie, puzzling, the hunt begins.
```

## 第五批：畫框（5 張，可選）

畫框目前是用程式畫的邊框，生成圖片的話會漂亮很多。ChatGPT 的透明背景常常不穩，所以請畫在純黑底上，中間留空就好，我會自己把中間挖空。

共用描述（每張都貼，再接後面的主題）：

```
An ornate decorative portrait frame, vertical 3:4, Viking Age Norse knotwork carving style, the center area completely empty and filled with solid pure black, the frame itself only around the edges about 8% of the width, flat front view, no text, no character inside.
```

| # | 檔名 | 畫框 | 主題 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 37 | f_frost | 霜狼畫框 | Made of pale blue ice and frost, two howling wolf heads at the top corners, snowflake runes. |
| 38 | f_hero | 英雄畫框 | Made of gold and dark carved oak, crossed axes and a round shield at the top, Urnes-style serpents. |
| 39 | f_raven | 渡鴉畫框 | Made of black iron and silver, two ravens at the top, runic inscriptions along the sides. |
| 40 | f_blood | 血色畫框 | Made of dark iron stained with deep red, jagged edges, battle scratches, a red sail motif. |
| 41 | f_gold | 金淚畫框 | Made of soft shining gold with flowing tear-drop shapes, small falcon feathers and the Brísingamen necklace woven in. |

---

### 優先順序

- **第一批（勇士）和第二批（第四章人物）**對遊戲體驗影響最大，建議先畫。
- **第三、四批**是錦上添花。
- **第五批**可有可無，不畫的話會繼續用現在的程式邊框。

## 補充：新勇士與諸神黃昏（4 張）

人物先貼「人物用」共用風格，場景先貼「場景用」共用風格，再接下面的描述。

| # | 檔名 | 內容 | 描述 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 44 | c_bjorn | 「鐵甲」比約恩（人物） | A young Viking berserker in his early twenties, son of the red-bearded berserker Ragnar, the same wild red hair and beard but younger and leaner, wearing a heavy ring-mail shirt that looks unbreakable, biting the rim of a battered shield, fierce grin, sea and longships behind him. |
| 45 | c_sigrid | 盾女西格麗德（人物） | A proud young shieldmaiden around twenty, a jarl's daughter, dark blonde hair in a thick braid, a richly painted round shield with her father's sigil, fine chainmail and a blue wool cloak, confident half-smile, a law stone and a crowd of onlookers behind her. |
| 46 | sc_einherjar | 英靈助戰（場景） | Ragnarök battlefield in endless snow under a burning red sky. From a golden break in the storm clouds, hundreds of glowing ghostly einherjar warriors of Valhalla ride and march down a shining rainbow bridge to join a small band of living Viking warriors with round shields. Ahead, frost giants and a giant wolf. Epic, awe-inspiring, hopeful in the darkness. |
| 47 | sc_ragfall | 倒在諸神黃昏（場景） | Aftermath of Ragnarök: a world of ash, ice and dying fire, a dark red sun sinking. A fallen Viking ruler lies in the ash with a broken shield; beside them, a weary Norse god kneels, and they look into each other's eyes with quiet despair. Ruined halls and a fallen world tree in the background. Tragic, solemn, beautiful. |
